SELECT 'D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD AS COLUMN_NAME , '''D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD || ''' AS D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD AS PV_NAME , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD || '_31' AS SORT_ORDER_NO FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A WHERE A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= :B2 AND A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= :B1 UNION ALL SELECT 'W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(A.ISO_WEEK,2,'0') AS COLUMN_NAME , '''W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(A.ISO_WEEK,2,'0') || ''' AS W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(A.ISO_WEEK,2,'0') AS PV_NAME , A.BASIS_START_DATE || '_21' AS SORT_ORDER_NO FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A WHERE A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= :B2 AND A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= :B3 GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM, A.ISO_WEEK, A.BASIS_START_DATE UNION ALL SELECT 'M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM AS COLUMN_NAME , '''M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || ''' AS M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM AS PV_NAME , MIN(A.BASIS_START_DATE) || '_01' AS SORT_ORDER_NO FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A WHERE A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= :B2 AND A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= :B3 GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM ORDER BY SORT_ORDER_NO

END OF STMT

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION (VERSION_NO, VERSION_NO_W, NOW_YYYYMMDD, SP_VERSION_NO) SELECT A.TOKEN AS VERSION_NO , CASE WHEN D.DM_RTF_YN = 'N' THEN A.TOKEN || '(' || C.ISO_YYYYWW || ') (NO PLAN)' ELSE A.TOKEN || '(' || C.ISO_YYYYWW || ')' END AS VERSION_NO_W , TO_CHAR(SCM_MGR.FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(B.YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD') AS NOW_YYYYMMDD , (SELECT MAX(V2.VERSION_NO) FROM SCM_MGR.DM_MST_VERSION V2 INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M CM ON V2.YYYYMMDD = CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD WHERE V2.VERSION_TYPE LIKE 'SP%' AND V2.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID AND CM.ISO_YYYYWW = C.ISO_YYYYWW) AS SP_VERSION_NO FROM (SELECT TOKEN FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN WHERE PARAM_NM = 'p_version_type') A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_MST_VERSION B ON B.VERSION_NO = A.TOKEN AND B.DIVISION_ID = :B1 INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C ON C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = B.YYYYMMDD LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCP_PLN_INTERFACE_YN D ON D.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID AND D.PLAN_START_YYYYMMDD = C.BASIS_START_DATE

END OF STMT

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE (MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID) WITH MEA1 AS ( SELECT U.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE , NVL(M.ATTRIBUTE5, U.COLUMN_NAME) AS RESULT_MEASURE , U.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE_GROUP , CAST(U.SORT_ORDER_NO AS NUMBER(10,2)) AS SORT_ORDER_NO , U.USER_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE M ON M.PROGRAM_ID = U.PROGRAM_ID AND M.SHEET_NAME = U.SHEET_NAME AND M.COLUMN_NAME = U.COLUMN_NAME AND M.VALID_YN = 'Y' WHERE U.PROGRAM_ID = :B3 AND U.SHEET_NAME = :B2 AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M' AND U.USER_ID = :B1 ), MEA2 AS ( SELECT S.MEASURE, S.MEASURE AS RESULT_MEASURE, 'CUST_FCST' AS MEASURE_GROUP , CAST(A.SORT_ORDER_NO + S.SORT_NO AS NUMBER(10,2)) AS SORT_ORDER_NO, A.USER_ID FROM (SELECT U.USER_ID, U.SORT_ORDER_NO FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U WHERE U.PROGRAM_ID = :B3 AND U.SHEET_NAME = :B2 AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M' AND U.USER_ID = :B1 AND U.COLUMN_NAME = 'CUST_FCST') A, (SELECT MEASURE, MIN(SORT_NO) AS SORT_NO FROM (SELECT 'CUST_FCST_EDI' AS MEASURE, 0.11 AS SORT_NO FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Backorder' , 0.12 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Commercial_Agreement' , 0.13 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Firm_Temp' , 0.14 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Immidiate' , 0.15 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Manumacturing_Material_Temp' , 0.16 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Material_Temp' , 0.17 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT 'Planning_Forecast' , 0.18 FROM DUAL UNION ALL SELECT C.MEASURE, 0.10 FROM SCM_MGR.DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST C WHERE C.MEASURE_GROUP = 'CUST_FCST' AND NVL(C.MEASURE, '/') <> '/' AND C.VALID_YN = 'Y') GROUP BY MEASURE) S WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM MEA1 X WHERE X.MEASURE = S.MEASURE) ), MEA3 AS ( SELECT M.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE, M.COLUMN_NAME AS RESULT_MEASURE, M.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE_GROUP , CAST(M.SORT_ORDER_NO AS NUMBER(10,2)) AS SORT_ORDER_NO, 'DM_ADMIN' AS USER_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE M WHERE M.PROGRAM_ID = :B3 AND M.SHEET_NAME = :B2 AND M.COLUMN_NAME IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'FCST_GAP', 'RTF_SYS_RP', 'MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW', 'SCM_FCST', 'MKT_FCST') AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM MEA1 X WHERE X.MEASURE = M.COLUMN_NAME) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM MEA2 X WHERE X.MEASURE = M.COLUMN_NAME) ) SELECT MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID FROM MEA1 UNION ALL SELECT MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID FROM MEA2 UNION ALL SELECT MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID FROM MEA3

END OF STMT

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_FCST (DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY) SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE, MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C ON C.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND C.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID WHERE (:B3 LIKE '81%' OR :B3 = '82') AND A.DIVISION_ID = :B3 AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE UNION ALL SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE, MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C ON C.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND C.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID WHERE (:B3 IS NULL OR (:B3 NOT LIKE '81%' AND :B3 <> '82')) AND A.DIVISION_ID = :B3 AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE

END OF STMT

PARSING IN CURSOR #139924470563328 len=9376 dep=1 uid=163 oct=3 lid=163 tim=5393492157606 hv=1878907091 ad='2f99defc8' sqlid='cjxra9trzvq6m'

WITH INTOG AS (
    SELECT B.USER_GROUP, B.DIVISION_ID, B.MEASURE, B.DAYS, B.FROM_TIME, B.TO_TIME, LM.TOKEN AS LIMIT_MEASURE
    FROM  (SELECT A.SEGMENT1 AS USER_GROUP, A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE, DT.TOKEN AS DAYS
                , A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE4 AS LIMIT_MEASURE
           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
           CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE1, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                               FROM   DUAL
                               CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE1, ','), 0) + 1) DT
           WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
           AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
           AND (A.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
             OR A.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
           AND    A.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
           AND    A.SEGMENT3 = '81'
          ) B
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                        FROM   DUAL
                        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, ','), 0) + 1) LM
    WHERE (REPLACE(B.DAYS, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.DAYS = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.FROM_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.FROM_TIME <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.TO_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.TO_TIME >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
), ADM0 AS (
    SELECT A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE
         , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.SEGMENT4) THEN 'Y'
                WHEN EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1
                    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL T
                    WHERE  T.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                    AND    T.VALID_YN = 'Y'
                    AND   (T.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
                           OR T.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
                    AND    T.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT3 = '81'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT4 = A.SEGMENT4
                    AND    REPLACE(T.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
               ) THEN 'T'
                ELSE 'N' END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
         , CASE WHEN A.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS TODAY_LIMIT_YN
         , A.ATTRIBUTE1 AS TODAY_DAY_LIST
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
    WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
    AND    A.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
    AND    A.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
    GROUP BY A.SEGMENT4, A.ATTRIBUTE1
), ADM AS (
    SELECT A.MEASURE, A.TODAY_LIMIT_YN, A.TODAY_DAY_LIST
         , CASE WHEN A.E4 = 'Y'
                 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE)
                 AND NOT EXISTS (
                     SELECT 1
                     FROM   INTOG B
                     WHERE  B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE
                     AND    NOT EXISTS (
                              SELECT 1
                              FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L2
                              WHERE  L2.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                              AND    L2.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT3    = '81'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT4    = B.MEASURE
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT1    = B.LIMIT_MEASURE
                              AND    REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                              AND    INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                           ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0
   
            AND    L2.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')))
                THEN 'N' ELSE A.E4 END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
    FROM (
        SELECT A.*
             , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1
                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                   CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(L.ATTRIBUTE4, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                                       FROM   DUAL
                                       CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ','), 0) + 1) LM
                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '81'
                   AND    REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                   ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0)
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE2, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE2 <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE3, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND    TRIM(LM.TOKEN) = A.MEASURE
                   AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L3
                                      WHERE  L3.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                      AND    L3.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT3    = '81'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT1    = TRIM(LM.TOKEN)))
                    THEN 'N' ELSE A.E3 END AS E4
        FROM (
            SELECT A.*
                 , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req'
                        THEN CASE WHEN '81' IN ('40', '82')
                                   AND NOT ( ( 'ADMIN' IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner')
                                               OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                          WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                          AND   G.USER_GR_ID IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y') )
                                             AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req') )
                                  THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                        ELSE A.E2 END AS E3
            FROM (
                SELECT A.*
                     , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'AS_In_Req'
                            THEN CASE WHEN ('81' LIKE '81%' OR '81' = '82')
                                       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                   WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                   AND   G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Planner (W/S)' AND G.USE_YN = 'Y')
                                      THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                            ELSE A.E1 END AS E2
                FROM (
                    SELECT A.*
                         , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'LOC'
                                 AND ( NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                                                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '81')
                                       OR ( '81' NOT LIKE '81%'
                       
       OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'SCP Planner'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') )
                                            AND NOT ( 'ADMIN' = 'DM Viewer'
                                                OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Viewer'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') ) ) )
                                THEN 'Y' ELSE A.EDIT_AUTH_YN END AS E1
                    FROM ADM0 A
                ) A
            ) A
        ) A
    ) A
) SELECT MEASURE, EDIT_AUTH_YN, TODAY_LIMIT_YN, TODAY_DAY_LIST FROM ADM ORDER BY MEASURE

END OF STMT

PARSING IN CURSOR #139924469795848 len=46153 dep=1 uid=163 oct=3 lid=163 tim=5393492277804 hv=690172177 ad='250dc8b00' sqlid='cx68dh8nk6c8j'

WITH INTOG AS (
    SELECT B.USER_GROUP, B.DIVISION_ID, B.MEASURE, B.DAYS, B.FROM_TIME, B.TO_TIME, LM.TOKEN AS LIMIT_MEASURE
    FROM  (SELECT A.SEGMENT1 AS USER_GROUP, A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE, DT.TOKEN AS DAYS
                , A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE4 AS LIMIT_MEASURE
           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
           CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE1, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                               FROM   DUAL
                               CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE1, ','), 0) + 1) DT
           WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
           AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
           AND (A.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
             OR A.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
           AND    A.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
           AND    A.SEGMENT3 = '81'
          ) B
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                        FROM   DUAL
                        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, ','), 0) + 1) LM
    WHERE (REPLACE(B.DAYS, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.DAYS = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.FROM_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.FROM_TIME <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
    AND   (REPLACE(B.TO_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
           OR B.TO_TIME >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
), ADM0 AS (
    SELECT A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE
         , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.SEGMENT4) THEN 'Y'
                WHEN EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1
                    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL T
                    WHERE  T.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                    AND    T.VALID_YN = 'Y'
                    AND   (T.SEGMENT1 = 'ADMIN'
                           OR T.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
                    AND    T.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT3 = '81'
                    AND    T.SEGMENT4 = A.SEGMENT4
                    AND    REPLACE(T.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
               ) THEN 'T'
                ELSE 'N' END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
         , CASE WHEN A.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS TODAY_LIMIT_YN
         , A.ATTRIBUTE1 AS TODAY_DAY_LIST
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
    WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
    AND    A.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
    AND    A.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
    GROUP BY A.SEGMENT4, A.ATTRIBUTE1
), ADM AS (
    SELECT A.MEASURE, A.TODAY_LIMIT_YN, A.TODAY_DAY_LIST
         , CASE WHEN A.E4 = 'Y'
                 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE)
                 AND NOT EXISTS (
                     SELECT 1
                     FROM   INTOG B
                     WHERE  B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE
                     AND    NOT EXISTS (
                              SELECT 1
                              FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L2
                              WHERE  L2.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                              AND    L2.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT3    = '81'
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT4    = B.MEASURE
                              AND    L2.SEGMENT1    = B.LIMIT_MEASURE
                              AND    REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                              AND    INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                           ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0
   
            AND    L2.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')))
                THEN 'N' ELSE A.E4 END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
    FROM (
        SELECT A.*
             , CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1
                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                   CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(L.ATTRIBUTE4, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
                                       FROM   DUAL
                                       CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ','), 0) + 1) LM
                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '81'
                   AND    REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
                                   ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0)
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE2, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE2 <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE3, ' ', '') IS NULL
                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
                   AND    TRIM(LM.TOKEN) = A.MEASURE
                   AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L3
                                      WHERE  L3.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                      AND    L3.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT3    = '81'
                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT1    = TRIM(LM.TOKEN)))
                    THEN 'N' ELSE A.E3 END AS E4
        FROM (
            SELECT A.*
                 , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req'
                        THEN CASE WHEN '81' IN ('40', '82')
                                   AND NOT ( ( 'ADMIN' IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner')
                                               OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                          WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                          AND   G.USER_GR_ID IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y') )
                                             AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM INTOG B WHERE B.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req') )
                                  THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                        ELSE A.E2 END AS E3
            FROM (
                SELECT A.*
                     , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'AS_In_Req'
                            THEN CASE WHEN ('81' LIKE '81%' OR '81' = '82')
                                       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                   WHERE G.USER_ID IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                   AND   G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Planner (W/S)' AND G.USE_YN = 'Y')
                                      THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
                            ELSE A.E1 END AS E2
                FROM (
                    SELECT A.*
                         , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'LOC'
                                 AND ( NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
                                                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
                                                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'DMF0082'
                                                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = '81')
                                       OR ( '81' NOT LIKE '81%'
                       
       OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'SCP Planner'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') )
                                            AND NOT ( 'ADMIN' = 'DM Viewer'
                                                OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
                                                           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
                                                           WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN ('ADMIN', 'k0101')
                                                           AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Viewer'
                                                           AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') ) ) )
                                THEN 'Y' ELSE A.EDIT_AUTH_YN END AS E1
                    FROM ADM0 A
                ) A
            ) A
        ) A
    ) A
)
, CAL AS (  /* L4768~4797 (주 달력 GTT 조인 WE 는 결과 무영향 — 제거) */
    SELECT D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
         , TO_CHAR(D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS BUCKET_KEY
         , 'D' AS BUCKET_TYPE
         , D.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM
         , D.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(TRIM(D.ISO_WEEK), 2, '0') AS YYYYMMWW
         , CASE WHEN WE2.WEEK_END_DATE  IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS WEEK_END_YN
         , CASE WHEN ME.MONTH_END_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS MONTH_END_YN
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAX(BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS WEEK_END_DATE
               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
               WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN '20261101' AND '20270430'
               GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, ISO_WEEK) WE2
        ON  WE2.WEEK_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAX(BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS MONTH_END_DATE
               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
               WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN '20261101' AND '20270430'
               GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM) ME
        ON  ME.MONTH_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
    WHERE  D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN '20261101' AND '20271017'
), PVT AS (  /* L4802~4815 */
    SELECT BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SUN' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SUN
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'MON' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS MON
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'TUE' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS TUE
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'WED' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS WED
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'THU' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS THU
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'FRI' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS FRI
         , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SAT' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SAT
    FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION BC
    GROUP BY BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS
)
, VD AS (  /* L4816~4822 */
    SELECT S.SCM_SITE_ID, MAX(S.VMI_YN || '^' || NVL(TO_CHAR(S.ATTRIBUTE5),'')) AS VMI_STD_DIO
    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S
    WHERE  S.DIVISION_ID = '82'
    AND    '81' = '82'
    GROUP BY S.SCM_SITE_ID
)
, MSP AS (
    SELECT F.DIVISION_ID, F.PROJECT_CODE, F.END_CUSTOMER, F.SCM_SITE_ID, F.ITEM_ID, F.DOM_EXP, F.FCST_TYPE, F.INCOTERMS, F.MEASURE, F.PLANT_SITE_ID, F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
         , CAL.BUCKET_TYPE, CAL.BUCKET_KEY, F.QTY AS QTY, F.MEASURE_GROUP
         , F.NICK_NAME AS NICK_NAME
         , F.DEPT_ID AS DEPT_ID
         , F.VERSION_NO AS VERSION_NO
         , F.SORT_ORDER_NO
         , NVL(F.AS_FROM_SITE, ' ') AS AS_FROM_SITE
         , F.CURRENCY AS CURRENCY
         , F.NOTATION AS NOTATION
         , CASE WHEN '81' = '82' THEN 'N'
              
M ADM MA
                    WHERE  MA.MEASURE = F.MEASURE
                    AND    MA.EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'Y'
                    AND   (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'N'
                           OR (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'Y'
                               AND INSTR(
                                       REPLACE(TRIM(MA.TODAY_DAY_LIST),' ',''),
                                       CASE TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'D'))
                                           WHEN 1 THEN 'SUN' WHEN 2 THEN 'MON'
                                           WHEN 3 THEN 'TUE' WHEN 4 THEN 'WED'
                                           WHEN 5 THEN 'THU' WHEN 6 THEN 'FRI'
                                           WHEN 7 THEN 'SAT'
                                       END) > 0))
                ) THEN 'Y'
                ELSE 'N'
           END AS EDIT_FLAG
         , '999' AS SORT_KEY
         , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(50)) AS GROUP_ID
         , PVT.SUN, PVT.MON, PVT.TUE, PVT.WED, PVT.THU, PVT.FRI, PVT.SAT
         , CAL.YYYYMM, CAL.YYYYMMWW, CAL.WEEK_END_YN, CAL.MONTH_END_YN
         , 'N' AS NEW_FLAG
         , 'N' AS AS_YN
         , VD.VMI_STD_DIO AS VMI_STD_DIO
         , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(4000)) AS ATTR1
    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE F
    INNER JOIN CAL ON CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = F.YYYYMMDD
    LEFT JOIN PVT ON  PVT.DIVISION_ID = F.DIVISION_ID
                  AND PVT.SCM_SITE_ID = F.SCM_SITE_ID
                  AND PVT.INCOTERMS   = F.INCOTERMS
    LEFT JOIN VD ON VD.SCM_SITE_ID = F.SCM_SITE_ID
    /* MSP 항등 경로: 11키×일 유일 실측 — GROUP BY 생략 */
)
, M1 AS (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, CASE WHEN X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = '/' OR (X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) IS NULL) THEN '-' ELSE X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID END AS CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.DEPT_ID, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, X.AS_FROM_SITE, X.CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1 FROM MSP X)
, M3R AS (SELECT NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'/') AS K1, NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'/') AS K2, NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'/') AS K3, NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'/') AS K4, NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'/') AS K5, NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'/') AS K6, NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'/') AS K7, NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'/') AS K8, NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'/') AS K9, NVL(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,'/') AS K10, MAX(X.AS_FROM_SITE) AS AFS_MX_ FROM M1 X WHERE X.MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST' AND RTRIM(X.AS_FROM_SITE, ' -/') IS NOT NULL GROUP BY NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'/'), NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'/'), NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'/'), NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'/'), NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'/'), NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'/'), NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'/'), NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'/'), NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'/'), NVL(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,'/') HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT X.AS_FROM_SITE) = 1)
, M3 AS (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.DEPT_ID, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, CASE WHEN X.MEASURE <> 'MKT_FCST' AND RTRIM(X.AS_FROM_SITE, ' -/') IS NULL AND R.AFS_MX_ IS NOT NULL THEN R.AFS_MX_ ELSE X.AS_FROM_SITE END AS AS_FROM_SITE, X.CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1 FROM M1 X LEFT JOIN M3R R ON (NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'/') = R.K1 AND NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'/') = R.K2 AND NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'/') = R.K3 AND NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'/') = R.K4 AND NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'/') = R.K5 AND NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'/') = R.K6 AND NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'/') = R.K7 AND NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'/') = R.K8 AND NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'/') = R.K9 AND NVL(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,'/') = R.K10))
, M
 X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.DEPT_ID, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, X.AS_FROM_SITE, CASE WHEN X.MEASURE NOT IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'USD', 'MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW') AND (X.CURRENCY IS NULL OR TRIM(X.CURRENCY) IS NULL OR TRIM(X.CURRENCY) = '/') THEN ' ' ELSE X.CURRENCY END AS CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1 FROM M3 X)
, M5R AS (SELECT NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'~') AS K1, NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'~') AS K2, NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'~') AS K3, NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'~') AS K4, NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'~') AS K5, NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'~') AS K6, NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'~') AS K7, NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'~') AS K8, NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'~') AS K9, NVL(X.MEASURE,'~') AS K10, MAX(X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) KEEP (DENSE_RANK LAST ORDER BY CASE WHEN X.BUCKET_KEY >= '20261102' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END, X.BUCKET_KEY) AS CP_REP_ FROM M4 X WHERE X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID IS NOT NULL GROUP BY NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'~'), NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'~'), NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'~'), NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'~'), NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'~'), NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'~'), NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'~'), NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'~'), NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'~'), NVL(X.MEASURE,'~') HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) > 1)
, M5 AS (SELECT X.DIVISION_ID, X.PROJECT_CODE, X.END_CUSTOMER, X.SCM_SITE_ID, X.ITEM_ID, X.DOM_EXP, X.FCST_TYPE, X.INCOTERMS, X.MEASURE, X.PLANT_SITE_ID, CASE WHEN R.CP_REP_ IS NOT NULL THEN R.CP_REP_ ELSE X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID END AS CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, X.BUCKET_TYPE, X.BUCKET_KEY, X.QTY, X.MEASURE_GROUP, X.NICK_NAME, X.DEPT_ID, X.VERSION_NO, X.SORT_ORDER_NO, X.AS_FROM_SITE, X.CURRENCY, X.NOTATION, X.EDIT_FLAG, X.SORT_KEY, X.GROUP_ID, X.SUN, X.MON, X.TUE, X.WED, X.THU, X.FRI, X.SAT, X.YYYYMM, X.YYYYMMWW, X.WEEK_END_YN, X.MONTH_END_YN, X.NEW_FLAG, X.AS_YN, X.VMI_STD_DIO, X.ATTR1 FROM M4 X LEFT JOIN M5R R ON (NVL(X.DIVISION_ID,'~') = R.K1 AND NVL(X.PROJECT_CODE,'~') = R.K2 AND NVL(X.END_CUSTOMER,'~') = R.K3 AND NVL(X.SCM_SITE_ID,'~') = R.K4 AND NVL(X.ITEM_ID,'~') = R.K5 AND NVL(X.DOM_EXP,'~') = R.K6 AND NVL(X.FCST_TYPE,'~') = R.K7 AND NVL(X.INCOTERMS,'~') = R.K8 AND NVL(X.PLANT_SITE_ID,'~') = R.K9 AND NVL(X.MEASURE,'~') = R.K10))
, SRCA AS (SELECT X.*, X.DEPT_ID AS TEAM_ID, NVL(TO_CHAR(X.SUN),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.MON),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.TUE),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.WED),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.THU),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.FRI),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(X.SAT),'0') AS BUCKET FROM M5 X)
, DISP AS (SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, SORT_ORDER_NO, YYYYMM, YYYYMMWW, WEEK_END_YN, MONTH_END_YN, BUCKET_KEY, SUM(X.QTY) AS QTY, COUNT(*) AS CNT_ FROM SRCA X WHERE ((X.MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP') AND X.QTY <> 0) OR X.MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP')) GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, SORT_ORDER_NO, YYYYMM, YYYYMMWW, WEEK_END_YN, MONTH_END_YN, BUCKET_KEY)
    SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE

    FROM (
        SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, SORT_ORDER_NO, 'D_' || BUCKET_KEY AS PIVOT_BUCKET, QTY
        FROM DISP

        UNION ALL

        SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, SORT_ORDER_NO, 'W_' || YYYYMMWW AS PIVOT_BUCKET
             , CASE WHEN MEASURE IN ('BALANCE','VMI_Inv','AS_Inv')
                    THEN CASE WHEN WEEK_END_YN = 'Y' THEN SUM(QTY) ELSE 0 END
                    ELSE SUM(QTY) END AS QTY
        FROM DISP
        WHERE MEASURE NOT IN ('KRW','USD','LOC')
         GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, A
, MEASURE

        UNION ALL

        SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, SORT_ORDER_NO, 'W_' || YYYYMMWW AS PIVOT_BUCKET, SUM(QTY) / SUM(CNT_) AS QTY
        FROM DISP
        WHERE MEASURE IN ('KRW','USD','LOC')
         GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_ORDER_NO, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, NOTATION, YYYYMMWW

        UNION ALL

        SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, SORT_ORDER_NO, 'M_' || YYYYMM AS PIVOT_BUCKET
             , CASE WHEN MEASURE IN ('BALANCE','VMI_Inv','AS_Inv')
                    THEN CASE WHEN MONTH_END_YN = 'Y' THEN SUM(QTY) ELSE 0 END
                    ELSE SUM(QTY) END AS QTY
        FROM DISP
        WHERE MEASURE NOT IN ('KRW','USD','LOC')
         GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_ORDER_NO, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, NOTATION, YYYYMM, MONTH_END_YN, MEASURE

        UNION ALL

        SELECT DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, SORT_ORDER_NO, 'M_' || YYYYMM AS PIVOT_BUCKET, SUM(QTY) / SUM(CNT_) AS QTY
        FROM DISP
        WHERE MEASURE IN ('KRW','USD','LOC')
         GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, TEAM_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, PLANT_SITE_ID, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_ORDER_NO, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AS_FROM_SITE, NOTATION, YYYYMM
    ) SRC
